It is written so the scaffold can be built directly from it. Decisions with a longer rationale live in the [ADR log](adr/README.md).\n6\t\n7\t## Overview\n8\t\n9\tThe site is a static, zero-backend, single-page React app served from GitHub Pages. There is no router, no global store and no data fetching. The architecture comes down to four seams:\n10\t\n11\t1. Typed content: all copy lives in TypeScript modules checked against one schema.\n12\t2. CSS-variable design tokens: five role colors per theme drive every surface.\n13\t3. An internal animation layer: the only place the two animation libraries are imported.\n14\t4. A build and deploy pipeline: Vite build, GitHub Actions, GitHub Pages.\n15\t\n16\tEverything else is conventional React composition with props flowing down.\n17\t\n18\t## Layers\n19\t\n20\tDependencies point down only. A layer may import from layers below it, never above.\n21\t\n22\t```text\n23\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n24\t| app/        main.tsx, App.tsx, Providers, Page.tsx                    |\n25\t|             Page.tsx is the ONLY file that imports content data       |\n26\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n27\t| sections/   Hero, About, Experience, Projects, Skills, Education,     |\n28\t|             Contact                      (props in, JSX out)          |\n29\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n30\t| layout/     Container, Section, SiteHeader, PillNav, Footer,          |\n31\t|             SkipLink, Wordmark                                        |\n32\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n33\t| components/ ui/ (CtaLink, Toast, ThemeToggle, icons)                  |\n34\t|             motion-primitives/ (vendored source, treated as owned)    |\n35\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n36\t| anim/       the internal animation layer: the only place that         |\n37\t|             imports motion/react and @react-spring/web               |\n38\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n39\t| hooks/  lib/                  | styles/ (tokens.css, base.css,       |\n40\t| (useActiveSection, useTheme,  |  index.css = tailwind + theme map)   |\n41\t|  cn, a11y)                    |                                      |\n42\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n43\t| content/ (typed TS data)      assets/ (images, fonts via Fontsource) |\n44\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n45\tBuild and deploy: vite.config.ts, lint boundary rules, GitHub Actions\n46\t```\n47\t\n48\t## Import rules\n49\t\n50\tOnly src/anim/ and src/components/motion-primitives/ may import motion/react or @react-spring/web.\n51\t\n52\tOnly src/app/Page.tsx imports content data.\n53\t\n54\t| Importer | May import | May not import |\n55\t|----------|-----------|----------------|\n56\t| sections/* | layout, components/ui, the anim barrel (src/anim/index.ts), content types (not data) | motion/react, @react-spring/*, components/motion-primitives/* directly, content data |\n57\t| layout/*, components/ui/* | the anim barrel, lib, hooks | sections, content data |\n58\t| components/motion-primitives/* | motion/react, lib/cn | everything else (vendor code, kept out of lint autofix churn) |\n59\t| anim/* | motion/react, @react-spring/web, components/motion-primitives/*, the token mirror in styles | sections, layout |\n60\t| app/Page.tsx | content data, sections | any animation library or anim internals |\n61\t\n62\tThese rules are enforced by oxlint's `no-restricted-imports` rule with per-folder overrides in `.oxlintrc.json`, covering dynamic `import()` and re-exports as well, so a violation fails CI rather than relying on review. Unit tests under `tests/unit` may import the libraries for assertions. A gesture library is not adopted up front. It would be added to the anim row only if a react-spring drag cannot be built well otherwise, after a React 19 compatibility check.\n63\t\n64\t## Folder structure\n65\t\n66\t```text\n67\trepo root\n68\t|-- public/                  URL-stable files only: favicon set, og image,\n69\t|                            resume.pdf (a phone-number-free export), robots.txt\n70\t|-- index.html               meta and social tags, inline theme-init script,\n71\t|                            noscript fallback content\n72\t|-- 404.html                 second HTML entry: the not-found page that\n73\t|                            GitHub Pages serves for unknown paths\n74\t|-- vite.config.ts           base from env, Tailwind plugin, imagetools, \"@\" alias\n75\t|-- .oxlintrc.json           import-boundary rules (see Import rules)\n76\t|-- .github/workflows/       ci.yml, deploy.yml\n77\t`-- src/\n78\t    |-- main.tsx\n79\t    |-- app/                 App.tsx, Page.tsx, Providers.tsx\n80\t    |-- sections/            one folder per section (Hero, About, Experience,\n81\t    |                        Projects, Skills, Education, Contact)\n82\t    |-- layout/              Container, Section, SiteHeader, PillNav,\n83\t    |                        Footer, SkipLink, Wordmark\n84\t    |-- components/\n85\t    |   |-- ui/              CtaLink, Toast, ThemeToggle, icons/ (inline SVG\n86\t    |   |                    brand marks and UI glyphs)\n87\t    |   `-- motion-primitives/   vendored, patched locally, logged in an ADR\n88\t    |-- anim/\n89\t    |   |-- index.ts         public barrel: the only animation import path\n90\t    |   |-- MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx\n91\t    |   |-- useMotionPreference.ts\n92\t    |   |-- tokens.ts        springs, durations, eases in both libraries' formats\n93\t    |   |-- MotionFeatures.tsx   LazyMotion provider with the async feature chunk\n94\t    |   |-- declarative/     motion-owned wrappers (ToastPresence,\n95\t    |   |                    NavIndicator, PillMorph, TextReveal)\n96\t    |   `-- physics/         react-spring-owned wrappers (ThemeThumb and other\n97\t    |                        pointer-driven, velocity-carrying helpers)\n98\t    |-- content/\n99\t    |   |-- schema.ts        every type\n100\t    |   |-- profile.ts, experience.ts, projects.ts, skills.ts,\n101\t    |   |   education.ts, contacts.ts     one module per section\n102\t    |   |-- images.ts        image registry\n103\t    |   `-- index.ts         assembles siteContent with satisfies\n104\t    |-- hooks/               useActiveSection, useTheme\n105\t    |-- lib/                 cn.ts, date formatting, a11y helpers\n106\t    |-- styles/\n107\t    |   |-- index.css        tailwind import, dark variant, theme mapping\n108\t    |   |-- tokens.css       role tokens, derived tokens, light and dark blocks\n109\t    |   `-- base.css         resets, focus ring, reduced-motion guard, fonts\n110\t    `-- assets/images/       hashed by Vite\n111\t```\n112\t\n113\tThe animation folder is named anim/ rather than motion/ so it is never confused with the motion package or its motion/react import path.\n114\t\n115\t## Data flow\n116\t\n117\tBuild time:\n118\t\n119\t```text\n120\tcontent/*.ts --(typed, satisfies)--> app/Page --props--> sections --props--> layout, ui\n121\tassets/*     --(imagetools, hashed imports)--> image fields in content\n122\t```\n123\t\n124\tRuntime, all client-side with no network use beyond same-origin assets:\n125\t\n126\t- Theme: an inline script in the document head reads localStorage, falls back to prefers-color-scheme, and sets the data-theme attribute on the html element before first paint. The theme provider in React only changes the attribute and storage on a user toggle.\n127\t- Motion preference: the OS reduced-motion setting (plus an optional site override) feeds one hook, useMotionPreference, which configures both animation libraries and the wrapper components. See Reduced motion.\n128\t- Scroll-spy: an IntersectionObserver over the section ids drives useActiveSection, which highlights the current section in the navigation.\n129\t- Pointer and touch input drive react-spring values written straight to the DOM, with no React re-render per frame.\n130\t- Project detail: a click or tap sets local state in the Projects section, which opens an overlay with focus handling.\n131\t\n132\tThere is no global state library. Three small contexts exist: theme, motion preference and, optionally, the active section. Everything else is props.\n133\t\n134\t## Content model\n135\t\n136\tAll types live in src/content/schema.ts:\n137\t\n138\t- Profile: name, tagline, bio paragraphs, optional quote, photo (ImageAsset), contacts.\n139\t- ContactLink: kind (email, github, linkedin or instagram), label, href, whether it is external.\n140\t- ExperienceEntry: id, role, org, optional org URL, optional logo, dates (DateRange), location, bullets, optional highlights and stack.\n141\t- Project: id, title, context kind (personal, employer, course or hackathon) with a label, dates, stack, bullets, relatedExperienceId, featured, optional links and image.\n142\t- EducationEntry: id, degree, school, location, dates, gpa, optional track.\n143\t- SkillGroup: id, label, items as a string array.\n144\t- DateRange: ISO start and end (or present) plus a display label in one normalized format.\n145\t- ImageAsset: src, alt, width, height, optional srcset.\n146\t\n147\tRules:\n148\t\n149\t- Content modules use satisfies against the schema so a missing field fails typecheck.\n150\t- Ordering lives in the data (array order or a priority field), never in components.\n151\t- Copy is taken verbatim from the reviewed content inventory; any edit is recorded there with before and after, not made silently in code.\n152\t- The featured flag decides what appears first on mobile, so \"key info in seconds\" is a data decision.\n153\t- Content tests check that ids are unique, related ids resolve, every image has alt text, every external link is https, and dates parse in descending order.\n154\t- A phone number is not a field anywhere in the model, and must never appear in site copy, metadata or the served resume.\n155\t\n156\t## Design tokens\n157\t\n158\t- Exactly five role tokens per theme: text, background, primary, secondary, accent. Light and dark are authored separately, not inverted.\n159\t- Derived tokens (surface, border, muted text, link, focus ring and so on) are computed from the five roles with color-mix(in oklch, ...). A palette swap therefore edits one block and every derived value follows.\n160\t- The theme is selected by the data-theme attribute on the html element.\n161\t- Tailwind v4 is configured CSS-first: a custom dark variant is bound to [data-theme=dark] with @custom-variant, and an @theme inline block maps utilities to the CSS variables. There is no tailwind.config.js.\n162\t- No raw hex values in components. Components use semantic utilities only; a CI check greps for stray hex.\n163\t- A contrast test recomputes every text, UI and focus pair in both themes against WCAG 2.2 AA (4.5:1 for body text, 3:1 for large text and UI) and fails the build on any miss.\n164\t- Spring and duration values that JavaScript needs are mirrored in CSS variables, with src/anim/tokens.ts as the canonical source for springs.\n165\t\n166\t## Animation layer\n167\t\n168\tsrc/anim/index.ts is the only animation import path for the rest of the app. It exposes a handful of semantic components and hooks, not library primitives.\n169\t\n170\t| Interaction class | Owner |\n171\t|-------------------|-------|\n172\t| Mount and unmount, presence, exit animations | motion |\n173\t| Layout and shared-element transitions | motion |\n174\t| Scroll-linked values and in-view reveals | motion |\n175\t| Text and number effects, stagger, ready-made motion-primitives prefabs | motion |\n176\t| Velocity-carrying physics: press-squish, rail hover lift with neighbor shift, theme toggle thumb | react-spring |\n177\t| Color and hover transitions, native scroll-snap rail swipe | CSS |\n178\t| Desktop wheel smoothing only | Lenis |\n179\t\n180\tThe rule: one library animates any given element. Never both libraries on one element or one property, and no Tailwind transition utilities on an element a library animates. If a vendored primitive duplicates a physics wrapper, one implementation is chosen per interaction and the other stays unused.\n181\t\n182\tSpring and duration tokens are defined once in src/anim/tokens.ts, exported in both libraries' formats (motion's stiffness and damping, react-spring's tension and friction), so the two feel like one system. Fine-pointer effects such as tilt, magnetic pull and spotlight are gated by (hover: hover) and (pointer: fine); touch gets a tap or press equivalent, or nothing. Every wrapper reads the motion preference, renders an instant equivalent when reduced, forwards className, and never hides content in its initial CSS state.\n183\t\n184\t## Reduced motion\n185\t\n186\tOne preference source, useMotionPreference, combines the OS prefers-reduced-motion setting with an optional site override and feeds three consumers:\n187\t\n188\t- motion: the provider renders MotionConfig with its reducedMotion setting. This disables transform and layout animation but not opacity or color, so wrappers handle parallax, scroll-linked and autoplay effects explicitly.\n189\t- react-spring: the provider calls Globals.assign({ skipAnimation }) so every spring jumps to its target; the effect cleans up on change.\n190\t- CSS: a @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) guard in base.css shortens transitions and animations and keeps scroll behavior native.\n191\t\n192\tContent is visible by default and is never hidden until JavaScript runs. Hidden initial states are applied client-side inside wrappers, and a noscript rule guarantees visibility. Pressed, focused and selected states must read without motion (color, outline, shape). Lenis is off under reduced motion. The provider listens for changes to the media query so a live OS toggle takes effect without a reload.\n193\t\n194\t## Assets\n195\t\n196\t- Images live in src/assets/images/ and are imported as modules (hashed, long-cached). vite-imagetools produces AVIF and WebP variants and srcset with intrinsic width and height to prevent layout shift. Below-the-fold images lazy-load; the hero image is high priority.\n197\t- Fonts are self-hosted through Fontsource (variable, latin subset, font-display swap), at most two families. No third-party font CDN.\n198\t- Brand icons (GitHub, LinkedIn, Instagram, email) and UI glyphs (close, chevrons, download) are inline SVG components under src/components/ui/icons/. No icon package is installed. See ADR-0005 and ADR-0010 in the ADR log.\n199\t- The resume PDF served from public/ is the owner's phone-number-free export of the resume. The phone-number gate reads its text layer, link annotations and metadata, and rejects a stand-in file.\n200\t- Only images that are actually used migrate from the previous site, with EXIF metadata stripped. Template leftovers are left behind.\n201\t\n202\t## Build and deploy\n203\t\n204\tGitHub Actions builds the site and publishes it to GitHub Pages. The Vite base comes from the VITE_BASE environment variable: the project URL path (for example /aa-phan-portfolio-v2/) while staging, and / after the cutover to the user-site URL. Assets are module-imported so no absolute asset paths exist; anything under public/ that JavaScript references goes through import.meta.env.BASE_URL. CI also builds with the project-path base so a broken sub-path is caught before launch. The build has a second HTML entry, 404.html, a static not-found page that GitHub Pages serves for unknown paths. Steps, settings, cutover and rollback are in the deploy runbook (deploy-runbook.md).\n205\t\n206\t## Quality gates\n207\t\n208\t| Gate | Command | Fails the build when |\n209\t|------|---------|----------------------|\n210\t| Typecheck | `tsc -b` | A content field is missing or a reference is wrong |\n211\t| Lint with import boundaries | oxlint, warnings denied | A section imports an animation library or content data |\n212\t| Format check | Prettier with Tailwind class sorting | A file is not formatted |\n213\t| Unit tests | Vitest: shell parity, theme logic, motion preference, gate negatives | Any test fails |\n214\t| Path-case check | `scripts/check-path-case.mjs`, run locally and on Linux CI | An import differs in case from the file on disk |\n215\t| Raw color check | `scripts/check-colors.mjs` | Source outside the token file and vendored primitives holds a raw hex, rgb, hsl, oklch or oklab value, or a Tailwind default palette utility |\n216\t| Token contrast test | `scripts/check-contrast.mjs` | Any text, UI or focus pair is below AA in any theme |\n217\t| Build | `vite build` | The Vite build errors |\n218\t| Initial JS budget | `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs` | The module script and modulepreloads exceed 150 kB gzipped |\n219\t| Phone-number check | `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs` over the built site | A served file holds a phone-shaped string or a tel: link, or a PDF's text layer, link annotations or metadata does (read with pdfjs-dist), or the PDF cannot be read, or resume.pdf is not a real resume (the owner's name and at least 1500 non-whitespace characters of text) |\n220\t| Image check | `scripts/check-images.mjs` over dist, public and src | A shipped raster is not AVIF or WebP, carries Exif or XMP, or is unreferenced, or a source image is not imported |\n221\t| Smoke tests | Playwright in CI | The hero is missing with JavaScript off, the theme flashes, or content is hidden or a transform animation runs with reduced motion emulated |\n222\t| axe | Later gate | Serious or critical violations in light or dark |\n223\t| Mobile Lighthouse | Later gate | Targets set in the scaffold milestone are missed |\n224\t\n225\t`npm run verify` chains the first eleven rows in that order, and both CI workflows call it.\n226\t\n227\t## Stack\n228\t\n229\tVerified 2026-10-08; re-verify at scaffold.\n230\t\n231\t| Technology | Version | Purpose |\n232\t|------------|---------|---------|\n233\t| Vite | ^8.3.4 | Build and dev server |\n234\t| React and React DOM | ^19.3.0 | UI |\n235\t| TypeScript | ~6.0.3 | Types |\n236\t| @vitejs/plugin-react | ^6.1.2 | React transform |\n237\t| Tailwind CSS and @tailwindcss/vite | ^4.3.3 | Styling, first-party Vite plugin |\n238\t| motion | ~14.0.0 | Declarative animation, imported from motion/react |\n239\t| @react-spring/web | ~10.1.2 | Spring physics |\n240\t| clsx | ^2.1.1 | Class composition |\n241\t| tailwind-merge | ^3.7.0 | Class conflict resolution |\n242\t| lenis | 1.3.x | Desktop wheel smoothing |\n243\t| vite-imagetools | ^12.0.1 | Image resize and format variants |\n244\t| pdfjs-dist | 6.3.289 (exact, dev only) | Phone-number gate: reads the served PDF's text, links and metadata |\n245\t| axe-core | 4.13.0 (dev only) | Accessibility pass of the visual QA run |\n246\t\n247\tUI glyphs are inline SVG (ADR-0010), so there is no icon package. Not used: the react-spring umbrella package, the react-spring v11 beta, raw framer-motion imports, shadcn init, tailwind.config.js, a client-side router, 3D or WebGL.\n248\t\n249\t## Decisions\n250\t\n251\tIndividual decisions, their status and the list of still-open decisions are tracked in the [ADR log](adr/README.md).\n252\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_019ZJmZ9GMBeutVA1YbMdMai","agent_id":"a5cf52b35cb0bd10c","session_id":"dca2f8eb-cfce-4808-a3a2-6118fb064725","uuid":"8dbc6366-451d-4058-9ac9-7dbbf00855f4","timestamp":"2026-11-20T04:50:11.340Z","tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_01Qddz6ErcfdarLposD6NeBs","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"rule"}}],"subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Map tech and arch codebase"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-5-5","id":"msg_011ChDaYaPG1mhjK9X5QcwRX","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01FHDG7gyYRCy1kFV9W6WqeF","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/private/var/folders/hl/ryh6jjw90v5gj8hg9j9clckm0000gn/T/avarice-eval-XEEEgF/T03-C-xQxeD1/arm/work/onb_01M7VMM5Z99KCQNMH7V6Z5ZSNN/staging/.planning/codebase/INTEGRATIONS.md","content":"# External Integrations\n\n**Analysis Date:** 2026-11-19\n\n## Summary\n\nThe site is a static, zero-backend single-page app. At runtime it makes no API calls, runs no analytics, and loads no third-party scripts, fonts, or stylesheets. The only external contact is outbound links the visitor chooses to follow. The only third-party service in the delivery path is GitHub (Actions and Pages).\n\n## APIs & External Services\n\n**Runtime:**\n- None. `src/` contains no `fetch(`, `XMLHttpRequest`, or SDK client. Content is bundled at build time from `src/content/`.\n\n**Outbound links (user-initiated navigation only):**\n- GitHub profile - `src/content/contacts.ts` (kind `github`)\n- LinkedIn profile - `src/content/contacts.ts` (kind `linkedin`)\n- Instagram profile - `src/content/contacts.ts` (kind `instagram`)\n- Project source repository (GitHub) - `src/content/projects.ts` (`links.code`), rendered as an external link with `target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"` in `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`\n- Email - a `mailto:` link built from the contact entry with kind `email` in `src/content/contacts.ts`. The same address is also hard-coded in the static hero markup in `index.html`. Keep the two in sync when the address changes.\n- All external content links must be `https` (checked by the content tests in `tests/unit/content.test.ts`, per `docs/architecture.md`).\n\n**Browser APIs used in place of services:**\n- Clipboard API `navigator.clipboard.writeText` with `document.execCommand('copy')` as fallback - `src/lib/copy.ts`. Used only for the public email address from a click handler. The site never reads the clipboard.\n- `window.location.href` assignment to open the mail client when copy fails - `src/lib/copy.ts` (`openMail`)\n\n## Data Storage\n\n**Databases:**\n- Not applicable. There is no database.\n\n**File Storage:**\n- Local filesystem only, at build time. Images are imported from `src/assets/images/` and hashed by Vite. The resume is served as a static file from `public/resume.pdf`.\n\n**Caching:**\n- None at the application layer. Static assets are long-cached by hashed filenames (Vite output).\n\n**Browser storage:**\n- `localStorage` key `theme` (constant `THEME_STORAGE_KEY` in `src/lib/theme.ts`). Stores only the explicit `light` or `dark` choice. Read before first paint by the inline script in `index.html`, and read and written by `src/lib/theme.ts`. Access is wrapped in try/catch because storage may be blocked. When blocked, the choice lasts for the page session only.\n- No cookies, no IndexedDB, no session storage use.\n\n## Authentication & Identity\n\n**Auth Provider:**\n- None. The site has no accounts, no login, and no protected routes.\n\n## Monitoring & Observability\n\n**Error Tracking:**\n- None. No Sentry, no error-reporting SDK.\n\n**Analytics:**\n- None detected. No analytics script in `index.html` or `src/`.\n\n**Logs:**\n- No runtime logging. Build and QA scripts print to the console (`scripts/*.mjs`).\n\n## CI/CD & Deployment\n\n**Hosting:**\n- GitHub Pages. Source and cutover details are in `docs/adr/0003-github-pages-source-and-cutover.md` and `docs/deploy-runbook.md`.\n- Project URL path while staging: `/aa-phan-portfolio-v2/` (`VITE_BASE` in the workflows). Cutover to the user-site root is planned.\n\n**CI Pipeline:**\n- GitHub Actions, two workflows:\n  - `.github/workflows/ci.yml` - runs on every `push` and `pull_request`. Steps: `npm ci`, `npm run verify`, `npx playwright install --with-deps chromium`, `npm run test:e2e`.\n  - `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` - runs on `push` to `main` and on `workflow_dispatch`. Three jobs:\n    - `build`: `npm ci`, `npm run verify`, `actions/configure-pages`, `actions/upload-pages-artifact` (uploads `./dist`)\n    - `e2e`: needs `build`; installs Chromium and runs `npm run test:e2e`\n    - `deploy`: needs `build` and `e2e`; `actions/deploy-pages` in the `github-pages` environment, then a `curl` of the published page checked for the owner's name\n- Actions are pinned to full commit SHAs with version comments (for example `actions/checkout@3d3c42e5...`). Keep that pinning when updating.\n- Permissions are least-privilege: workflows default to `contents: read`. `pages: write` and `id-token: write` are granted only to the `deploy` job.\n- Concurrency: `ci.yml` cancels in-progress runs per ref. `deploy.yml` uses group `pages` with `cancel-in-progress: false`.\n\n**Third-party actions used:**\n- `actions/checkout`, `actions/setup-node`, `actions/configure-pages`, `actions/upload-pages-artifact`, `actions/deploy-pages`\n- Playwright browser download via `npx playwright install --with-deps chromium` (from the Playwright CDN, at CI time only)\n\n## Environment Configuration\n\n**Required env vars (build time):**\n- `VITE_BASE` - Vite base path. Set in both workflows. Local default `/` when unset (`vite.config.ts`).\n- `CI` - read implicitly by Playwright and tooling; not referenced directly in `src/`.\n\n**Runtime env vars:**\n- None. The browser bundle reads only `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` (Vite-injected).\n\n**Secrets location:**\n- No application secrets. GitHub Actions uses only the built-in `GITHUB_TOKEN` and OIDC token (`id-token: write`) that `actions/deploy-pages` requires. No repository secrets are referenced in the workflow files.\n- No `.env` file is read by the build code, and no `.env` values are referenced in `src/` or `vite.config.ts` beyond the `loadEnv` call.\n\n## Quality Gates That Touch Outside Resources\n\n- `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs` - scans the built `dist/` output and the served `resume.pdf` for phone-shaped strings and `tel:` links. It reads the PDF with `pdfjs-dist`, which is local and makes no network calls.\n- `scripts/check-images.mjs` - checks shipped rasters for AVIF or WebP format and stripped EXIF/XMP metadata.\n- `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs` - checks the initial JS bundle size against 150 kB gzipped.\n\nNone of these scripts call a network service.\n\n## Webhooks & Callbacks\n\n**Incoming:**\n- None.\n\n**Outgoing:**\n- None.\n\n---\n\n*Integration audit: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3805,"cache_read_input_tokens":68208,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3805,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_019ZJmZ9GMBeutVA1YbMdMai","agent_id":"a5cf52b35cb0bd10c","session_id":"dca2f8eb-cfce-4808-a3a2-6118fb064725","uuid":"1ebd5997-da89-428f-8a59-ebf9b9dfd8ac","timestamp":"2026-11-20T04:51:35.127Z","request_id":"req_011ChDaYZpH4kJjFbe9jDK8f","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Map tech and arch codebase"}

refreshed: 2026-11-19 -->\n# Architecture\n\n**Analysis Date:** 2026-11-19\n\n## System Overview\n\n```text\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ `src/anim/index.ts`)             │\n│ motion/react (declarative)  |  @react-spring/web (physics)           │\n│ + vendored `src/components/motion-primitives/` `src/hooks/`, `src/lib/`, `src/styles/`, `src/content/`,    │\n│ `src/assets/images/`, `public/` Bootstrap | Mount React root in StrictMode, load the variable font and global CSS | `src/main.tsx` |\n| App root | Compose providers and the page | `src/app/App.tsx` |\n| Providers | Motion preference, lazy motion features, toast context | `src/app/Providers.tsx` |\n| Page | Assemble content into sections; wire scroll-spy into navigation | `src/app/Page.tsx` |\n| Content assembly | Bundle typed content into one `siteContent` object | `src/content/index.ts` |\n| Content schema | All content types | `src/content/schema.ts` |\n| Scroll-spy | IntersectionObserver over section ids, returns `activeId` and `lockTo` | `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` |\n| Theme state | Read and write `html[data-theme]` and `localStorage`; subscribe to changes | `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts` |\n| Theme inline script | Set `data-theme` before first paint | `index.html` (inline `<script>` in `<head>`) |\n| Motion preference | Combine OS reduced-motion with optional override | `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx` |\n| Lazy motion features | Load the `motion` feature bundle as a separate chunk | `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`, `src/anim/features.ts` |\n| Animation tokens | Spring and duration values for both libraries | `src/anim/tokens.ts` |\n| Declarative wrappers | Presence, nav indicator, pill morph, text reveal | `src/anim/declarative/` |\n| Physics wrappers | Velocity-based theme thumb | `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx` |\n| Vendored primitives | Owned copy of motion-primitives code | `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx` |\n| Toasts | Toast context and presentation | `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/useToast.ts` |\n| Copy-email | Clipboard write with fallback to mail link | `src/lib/copy.ts`, `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts` |\n| Project detail | Native `<dialog>` overlay with prev/next and focus restore | `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` |\n| Projects list | Project cards that open the dialog | `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`, `src/sections/Projects/Sleeve.tsx` |\n| Design tokens | Five role colors per theme, derived tokens via `color-mix` | `src/styles/tokens.css` |\n| Tailwind entry | Tailwind import, dark variant, theme mapping | `src/styles/index.css` |\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Static, client-only, layered React single-page app. Typed content flows down through props. There is no router, no global store, and no network data layer.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- One route. `Page.tsx` renders every section in one `<main>`. Navigation is anchor-based (`id` on each section, `scroll-mt` offsets in CSS).\n- Build-time content. All copy is in `src/content/*.ts`, typed with `satisfies` against `schema.ts`. Changes need a rebuild.\n- Enforced layering. Import rules are checked by oxlint `no-restricted-imports` in `.oxlintrc.json`, so a violation fails `npm run lint`.\n- One animation seam. Only `src/anim/` and the vendored primitive may import `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web`. Everything else imports from the `@/anim` barrel.\n- Token-driven theming. Components use semantic Tailwind utilities that resolve to CSS variables. No raw hex in components (`scripts/check-colors.mjs`).\n- Progressive enhancement. `index.html` contains static fallback markup inside `#root` so the hero is visible with JavaScript off. The `noscript` rule keeps content visible.\n\n## Layers\n\n**Application shell (`app/`):**\n- Purpose: Compose providers, load content, render the page, and wire scroll-spy into the navigation.\n- Location: `src/app/`\n- Contains: `App.tsx`, `Providers.tsx`, `Page.tsx`\n- Depends on: `@/content`, `@/hooks/useActiveSection`, `@/layout/*`, `@/sections/*`, `@/anim` (through `Providers.tsx`), `@/components/ui/Toast`\n- Used by: `src/main.tsx`\n- Note: `Page.tsx` is the only file allowed to import `@/content` (enforced in `.oxlintrc.json`).\n\n**Sections (`sections/`):**\n- Purpose: One folder per portfolio section. Each takes data as props and returns JSX.\n- Location: `src/sections/Hero/`, `Experience/`, `Projects/`, `About/`, `Skills/`, `Education/`, `Contact/`\n- Contains: section components, plus `Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`, `Projects/Sleeve.tsx`, `Projects/project-types.ts`\n- Depends on: `@/layout/*`, `@/components/ui/*`, `@/anim` barrel, `@/lib/*`, `@/hooks/*`, content types (`@/content` types only, not data)\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**Layout (`layout/`):**\n- Purpose: Page chrome and reusable containers.\n- Location: `src/layout/`\n- Contains: `Container.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `SiteHeader.tsx` (desktop nav), `PillNav.tsx` (phone pill nav), `Footer.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS`)\n- Depends on: `@/anim` barrel, `@/lib/*`, `@/components/ui/*`\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`, `src/sections/*`\n\n**Components (`components/`):**\n- Purpose: Reusable UI primitives, icons, and the vendored animation primitive.\n- Location: `src/components/ui/` (including `icons/` with inline SVG), `src/components/motion-primitives/`\n- Contains: `CtaLink.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `useToast.ts`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, icon components\n- Depends on: `@/anim` barrel, `@/lib/*`\n- Used by: sections and layout\n\n**Animation (`anim/`):**\n- Purpose: The only place that talks to `motion/react` and `@react-spring/web` (aside from the vendored primitive). Exposes semantic components and hooks.\n- Location: `src/anim/`\n- Contains: `index.ts` (barrel), `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `useMotionPreference.ts`, `MotionFeatures.tsx`, `features.ts`, `featuresStatus.ts`, `tokens.ts`, `declarative/`, `physics/`\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`, `src/components/motion-primitives/`, CSS token mirror\n- Must not import: `@/sections`, `@/layout`, `@/content` (enforced by an `anim/**` override in `.oxlintrc.json`)\n- Used by: layout, sections, components\n\n**Support (`hooks/`, `lib/`, `styles/`, `content/`, `assets/`):**\n- Purpose: Stateless helpers, theme logic, CSS tokens, typed data, and images.\n- Location: `src/hooks/` (`useActiveSection.ts`, `useTheme.ts`), `src/lib/` (`theme.ts`, `copy.ts`, `utils.ts`, `copy.ts` copy strings), `src/styles/` (`tokens.css`, `base.css`, `index.css`, `interactions.css`, `not-found.css`), `src/content/`, `src/assets/images/`\n- Depends on: nothing above it\n- Used by: all higher layers\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (page load)\n\n1. Browser parses `index.html`. The inline script in `<head>` reads `localStorage['theme']` and falls back to `prefers-color-scheme`, then sets `data-theme` on `<html>` before first paint (`index.html`).\n2. The module script `src/main.tsx` loads the variable font and `styles/index.css`, then renders `<App />` into `#root` (`src/main.tsx`). The static fallback markup in `#root` is replaced on mount.\n3. `App` renders `Providers` (`src/app/Providers.tsx`): `MotionPreferenceProvider` -> `MotionFeatures` -> `ToastProvider`.\n4. `Page` reads `siteContent` (`src/content/index.ts`), derives the single email address and the non-email contact links, and calls `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)` (`src/app/Page.tsx`).\n5. `Page` renders `SiteHeader` and `PillNav` (both receive `activeId` and `lockTo`), then the sections in order, then `Footer` (`src/app/Page.tsx`).\n6. `MotionFeatures` schedules a background import of `src/anim/features.ts` after first paint (`requestIdleCallback`, or a 1.5 s timer). It is skipped when reduced motion is on.\n\n### Scroll-spy\n\n1. `useActiveSection` creates one `IntersectionObserver` over the section ids, observing a thin band under the nav (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`).\n2. The observer sets `activeId`. A click on navigation calls `lockTo`, which holds the chosen id until scrolling settles.\n3. Desktop and phone navigation both read the same `activeId`, so there is one scroll-spy.\n\n### Theme toggle\n\n1. `ThemeToggle` (`src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`) calls `applyTheme` (`src/lib/theme.ts`).\n2. `applyTheme` writes `data-theme` first, then `localStorage`. If storage is blocked, it keeps an in-memory `unsavedChoice` flag.\n3. `subscribeTheme` uses a `MutationObserver` on `data-theme`, a `storage` listener for other tabs, and a `matchMedia` change listener for the OS scheme. It keeps `html[data-theme]` as the single source of truth.\n4. The visual thumb is `ThemeThumb` (`src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`), a react-spring component.\n\n### Project detail\n\n1. A click or tap on a project in `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx` or `Sleeve.tsx` sets a local `index` state.\n2. `ProjectDialog` (`src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`) calls `showModal()` in an effect and remembers the opener element.\n3. Previous, Next, and unmodified ArrowLeft and ArrowRight move through projects with wrap-around.\n4. On close, the index returns to `null` and focus returns to the opener.\n\n### Copy email\n\n1. `useCopyEmail` (`src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`) calls `copyText` (`src/lib/copy.ts`).\n2. `copyText` tries `navigator.clipboard.writeText`, then `document.execCommand('copy')` on a hidden textarea.\n3. If both fail, `openMail` assigns `window.location.href` to the `mailto:` link.\n4. Result text is shown through `useToast` and the toast layer.\n\n### Build time\n\n```text\nsrc/content/*.ts --(typed, satisfies)--> src/app/Page.tsx --props--> sections --props--> layout, components/ui\nsrc/assets/images/* --(vite-imagetools, hashed imports)--> image fields in content\npublic/resume.pdf --(import.meta.env.BASE_URL)--> same-origin download links\n```\n\n**State Management:**\n- No global store. Three React contexts: `MotionPreferenceProvider`, `ToastProvider`, and the theme state read through `useTheme` (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`).\n- DOM state: `html[data-theme]` is the theme source of truth. `dialog.open` is the project-detail source of truth.\n- Module-level state: `unsavedChoice` in `src/lib/theme.ts`, and a features-loaded flag in `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts`.\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**Content record:**\n- Purpose: Typed description of one piece of portfolio content.\n- Examples: `src/content/schema.ts` (types `Profile`, `Project`, `ExperienceEntry`, `EducationEntry`, `SkillGroup`, `ContactLink`, `DateRange`, `ImageAsset`, `Writeup`)\n- Pattern: Each `src/content/<section>.ts` exports a typed array; `src/content/index.ts` assembles them. Ordering lives in data, not components.\n\n**Motion preference:**\n- Purpose: One place that decides whether animation runs.\n- Examples: `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`\n- Pattern: The provider configures `motion`'s `MotionConfig`, calls react-spring's `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation })`, and listens to the media query so live OS changes apply without a reload.\n\n**Semantic animation wrappers:**\n- Purpose: Hide library choice from sections.\n- Examples: `src/anim/declarative/ToastPresence.tsx`, `src/anim/declarative/NavIndicator.tsx`, `src/anim/declarative/PillMorph.tsx`, `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx`, `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`\n- Pattern: Each wrapper reads the motion preference, renders an instant equivalent when reduced, forwards `className`, and never hides content in its initial CSS state.\n\n**Lazy feature bundle:**\n- Purpose: Keep the initial JS under budget.\n- Examples: `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx` (loader and idle warm-up), `src/anim/features.ts` (`domMax` feature set)\n- Pattern: `LazyMotion` gets a loader promise. A failed load returns a promise that never settles, so the `m` components stay static and no unhandled rejection is raised.\n\n**Design token mirror:**\n- Purpose: One palette edit updates the whole UI.\n- Examples: `src/styles/tokens.css` (five role tokens per theme, derived tokens via `color-mix(in oklch, ...)`), `src/anim/tokens.ts` (spring values in motion and react-spring formats)\n- Pattern: Light and dark are authored separately. Tailwind v4 maps utilities through `@theme inline`. The dark variant is bound with `@custom-variant` to `[data-theme=dark]`.\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**Browser document:**\n- Location: `index.html` (main), `404.html` (not-found entry)\n- Triggers: Any navigation to the site. GitHub Pages serves `404.html` for unknown paths.\n- Responsibilities: Static fallback content, theme pre-paint script, module script `src/main.tsx`\n\n**Module entry:**\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: Module script in `index.html`\n- Responsibilities: Font import, global CSS import, `createRoot`, render `<App />` in `StrictMode`\n\n**Build entry:**\n- Location: `vite.config.ts`\n- Triggers: `npm run build`, `npm run dev`, `npm run test:e2e`\n- Responsibilities: `base` from `VITE_BASE`, React, Tailwind, imagetools plugins, `@` alias, two HTML inputs\n\n**Verification entry:**\n- Location: `package.json` script `verify`\n- Triggers: `npm run verify`, run by both GitHub Actions workflows\n- Responsibilities: Chains typecheck, lint, format check, unit tests, and the QA gates in `scripts/`\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers. Pointer effects write react-spring values directly to the DOM without React re-renders per frame.\n- **Global state:** Module-level mutable state in `src/lib/theme.ts` (`unsavedChoice`) and `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts` (features-loaded flag). The theme attribute on `document.documentElement` is shared state written by several modules.\n- **Circular imports:** None observed in `src/`.\n- **Single route:** No router. Adding a second page means adding a router, and `index.html` plus `404.html` are both static HTML entries. Read `docs/adr/0003-github-pages-source-and-cutover.md` before changing routing.\n- **Base path:** `VITE_BASE` sets the Vite `base`. Every reference to a file under `public/` must go through `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` (see `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`, `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`).\n- **Import boundaries:** `src/anim/` must not import sections, layout, or content. Only `src/app/Page.tsx` imports content data. These are lint errors.\n- **Lenis is not installed:** `docs/architecture.md` describes Lenis for desktop wheel smoothing. `lenis` is not in `package.json` and is not imported under `src/`. The current code has no smooth-scroll library.\n- **Native dialog:** Project detail uses `<dialog>` with `showModal()`, not a portal (`docs/adr/0009-native-dialog-for-project-details.md`).\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Hard-coded copy outside the content model\n\n**What happens:** `index.html` repeats hero copy as static markup inside `#root`. This includes the tagline, the graduation line, the \"Last role\" line, and a `mailto:` link with the email address.\n**Why it's wrong:** The content model says copy lives in `src/content/` and changes are recorded in the content inventory. The static copy can drift from `src/content/profile.ts` and `src/content/contacts.ts`. The no-JS fallback is intentional, but the duplication is not checked against the content tests.\n**Do this instead:** When editing hero or contact copy, update `src/content/*` first, then mirror the fallback in `index.html` in the same change. Keep `docs/architecture.md`'s content rules in mind. The fallback exists only for the no-JS and first-paint case.\n\n### Documented library that is not installed\n\n**What happens:** `docs/architecture.md` lists `lenis` (1.3.x) in the stack table and in the animation layer table. The package is absent from `package.json`, and no source file imports it.\n**Why it's wrong:** A planner reading the architecture doc may assume smooth wheel scrolling exists, or may add a second scroll library. The rule \"one library animates any given element\" still applies, so adding Lenis would need a new ADR.\n**Do this instead:** Treat Lenis as planned. Confirm `package.json` before depending on it. Use native scroll behavior and `scroll-mt-(--nav-h)` for anchor offsets (see `src/app/Page.tsx` section markup and `index.html`).\n\n### Duplicated theme-init logic\n\n**What happens:** The pre-paint theme script in `index.html` and the runtime logic in `src/lib/theme.ts` both read `localStorage['theme']` and `prefers-color-scheme`. The two implementations must agree on the storage key and fallback rules.\n**Why it's wrong:** A change in one place (for example, a new theme value) breaks the other silently. The `tests/unit/theme-script.test.ts` file exists to catch parity drift, so run it after changing either.\n**Do this instead:** Change `src/lib/theme.ts` and `index.html` together, then run `npx vitest run tests/unit/theme-script.test.ts`.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Fail soft. Browser APIs are wrapped in try/catch and fall back to a visible, working state rather than throwing.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- Storage access (`localStorage`) is wrapped in try/catch. Blocked storage keeps the choice in memory (`src/lib/theme.ts`).\n- `matchMedia` is wrapped. If missing, the OS scheme is not followed and the theme is light (`src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`).\n- Clipboard failure falls back to `execCommand`, then to opening the mail client (`src/lib/copy.ts`).\n- A failed motion-feature chunk leaves animations static and raises no unhandled rejection (`src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`).\n- `IntersectionObserver` missing means scroll-spy does nothing and `activeId` stays `null` (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`).\n- There is no error boundary, and no runtime error reporting.\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None at runtime. Build and QA scripts log to the console.\n**Validation:** Compile-time only. `satisfies` against `src/content/schema.ts`, plus content unit tests (`tests/unit/content.test.ts`, `tests/unit/content-inventory.test.ts`). No runtime validation of data.\n**Authentication:** Not applicable.\n**Accessibility:** Skip link (`src/layout/SkipLink.tsx`), native dialog focus management, `prefers-reduced-motion` handling in `src/anim/` and `src/styles/base.css`, and axe checks in the QA loop.\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3175,"cache_read_input_tokens":72013,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3175,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_019ZJmZ9GMBeutVA1YbMdMai","agent_id":"a5cf52b35cb0bd10c","session_id":"dca2f8eb-cfce-4808-a3a2-6118fb064725","uuid":"ddadc988-9200-4ef2-bf09-41ea96f2f6c6","timestamp":"2026-11-20T04:52:11.596Z","request_id":"req_011ChDaZaLomgWeQMLGF29p7","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Map tech and arch codebase"}

refreshed: 2026-11-19 -->\n# Architecture\n\n**Analysis Date:** 2026-11-19\n\n## System Overview\n\n```text\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                 Browser: static SPA (no server, no API)              │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                                   │\n                                   ▼\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Entry + app shell                                                   │\n│  `src/main.tsx` -> `src/app/App.tsx` ->                 │ props                 │ props\n          ▼                          ▼                       ▼\n┌────────────────────┐  ┌─────────────────────────┐  ┌────────────────────┐\n│ Sections CtaLink, Toast,    │\n│ Projects, About,   │  │ Footer, SkipLink,       │  │ ThemeToggle, icons │\n│ Skills, Education, │  │ Container, Section,     │  │                    │\n│ Contact            │  │ Wordmark                │  │                    │\n└────────────────────┘  └─────────────────────────┘  └────────────────────┘\n          │                          │                       │\n          └──────────────┬───────────┴───────────────────────┘\n                         ▼\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ Animation layer `src/anim/` (barrel `src/anim/index.ts`)             │\n│ motion/react (declarative)  |  @react-spring/web (physics)           │\n│ + vendored `src/components/motion-primitives/`                       │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n          │\n          ▼\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ Support: `src/hooks/`, `src/lib/`, `src/styles/`, `src/content/`,    │\n│ `src/assets/images/`, `public/`                                      │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n## Component Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Responsibility | File |\n|-----------|----------------|------|\n| Bootstrap | Mount React root in StrictMode, load the variable font and global CSS | `src/main.tsx` |\n| App root | Compose providers and the page | `src/app/App.tsx` |\n| Providers | Motion preference, lazy motion features, toast context | `src/app/Providers.tsx` |\n| Page | Assemble content into sections; wire scroll-spy into navigation | `src/app/Page.tsx` |\n| Content assembly | Bundle typed content into one `siteContent` object | `src/content/index.ts` |\n| Content schema | All content types | `src/content/schema.ts` |\n| Scroll-spy | IntersectionObserver over section ids, returns `activeId` and `lockTo` | `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` |\n| Theme state | Read and write `html[data-theme]` and `localStorage`; subscribe to changes | `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts` |\n| Theme inline script | Set `data-theme` before first paint | `index.html` (inline `<script>` in `<head>`) |\n| Motion preference | Combine OS reduced-motion with optional override | `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx` |\n| Lazy motion features | Load the `motion` feature bundle as a separate chunk | `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`, `src/anim/features.ts` |\n| Animation tokens | Spring and duration values for both libraries | `src/anim/tokens.ts` |\n| Declarative wrappers | Presence, nav indicator, pill morph, text reveal | `src/anim/declarative/` |\n| Physics wrappers | Velocity-based theme thumb | `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx` |\n| Vendored primitives | Owned copy of motion-primitives code | `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx` |\n| Toasts | Toast context and presentation | `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/useToast.ts` |\n| Copy-email | Clipboard write with fallback to mail link | `src/lib/copy.ts`, `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts` |\n| Project detail | Native `<dialog>` overlay with prev/next and focus restore | `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` |\n| Projects list | Project cards that open the dialog | `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`, `src/sections/Projects/Sleeve.tsx` |\n| Design tokens | Five role colors per theme, derived tokens via `color-mix` | `src/styles/tokens.css` |\n| Tailwind entry | Tailwind import, dark variant, theme mapping | `src/styles/index.css` |\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Static, client-only, layered React single-page app. Typed content flows down through props. There is no router, no global store, and no network data layer.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- One route. `Page.tsx` renders every section in one `<main>`. Navigation is anchor-based (`id` on each section, `scroll-mt` offsets in CSS).\n- Build-time content. All copy is in `src/content/*.ts`, typed with `satisfies` against `schema.ts`. Changes need a rebuild.\n- Enforced layering. Import rules are checked by oxlint `no-restricted-imports` in `.oxlintrc.json`, so a violation fails `npm run lint`.\n- One animation seam. Only `src/anim/` and the vendored primitive may import `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web`. Everything else imports from the `@/anim` barrel.\n- Token-driven theming. Components use semantic Tailwind utilities that resolve to CSS variables. No raw hex in components (`scripts/check-colors.mjs`).\n- Progressive enhancement. `index.html` contains static fallback markup inside `#root` so the hero is visible with JavaScript off. The `noscript` rule keeps content visible.\n\n## Layers\n\n**Application shell (`app/`):**\n- Purpose: Compose providers, load content, render the page, and wire scroll-spy into the navigation.\n- Location: `src/app/`\n- Contains: `App.tsx`, `Providers.tsx`, `Page.tsx`\n- Depends on: `@/content`, `@/hooks/useActiveSection`, `@/layout/*`, `@/sections/*`, `@/anim` (through `Providers.tsx`), `@/components/ui/Toast`\n- Used by: `src/main.tsx`\n- Note: `Page.tsx` is the only file allowed to import `@/content` (enforced in `.oxlintrc.json`).\n\n**Sections (`sections/`):**\n- Purpose: One folder per portfolio section. Each takes data as props and returns JSX.\n- Location: `src/sections/Hero/`, `Experience/`, `Projects/`, `About/`, `Skills/`, `Education/`, `Contact/`\n- Contains: section components, plus `Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`, `Projects/Sleeve.tsx`, `Projects/project-types.ts`\n- Depends on: `@/layout/*`, `@/components/ui/*`, `@/anim` barrel, `@/lib/*`, `@/hooks/*`, content types (`@/content` types only, not data)\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**Layout (`layout/`):**\n- Purpose: Page chrome and reusable containers.\n- Location: `src/layout/`\n- Contains: `Container.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `SiteHeader.tsx` (desktop nav), `PillNav.tsx` (phone pill nav), `Footer.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS`)\n- Depends on: `@/anim` barrel, `@/lib/*`, `@/components/ui/*`\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`, `src/sections/*`\n\n**Components (`components/`):**\n- Purpose: Reusable UI primitives, icons, and the vendored animation primitive.\n- Location: `src/components/ui/` (including `icons/` with inline SVG), `src/components/motion-primitives/`\n- Contains: `CtaLink.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `useToast.ts`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, icon components\n- Depends on: `@/anim` barrel, `@/lib/*`\n- Used by: sections and layout\n\n**Animation (`anim/`):**\n- Purpose: The only place that talks to `motion/react` and `@react-spring/web` (aside from the vendored primitive). Exposes semantic components and hooks.\n- Location: `src/anim/`\n- Contains: `index.ts` (barrel), `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `useMotionPreference.ts`, `MotionFeatures.tsx`, `features.ts`, `featuresStatus.ts`, `tokens.ts`, `declarative/`, `physics/`\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`, `src/components/motion-primitives/`, CSS token mirror\n- Must not import: `@/sections`, `@/layout`, `@/content` (enforced by an `anim/**` override in `.oxlintrc.json`)\n- Used by: layout, sections, components\n\n**Support (`hooks/`, `lib/`, `styles/`, `content/`, `assets/`):**\n- Purpose: Stateless helpers, theme logic, CSS tokens, typed data, and images.\n- Location: `src/hooks/` (`useActiveSection.ts`, `useTheme.ts`), `src/lib/` (`theme.ts`, `copy.ts` with its user-facing strings, `utils.ts` with `cn`), `src/styles/` (`tokens.css`, `base.css`, `index.css`, `interactions.css`, `not-found.css`), `src/content/`, `src/assets/images/`\n- Depends on: nothing above it\n- Used by: all higher layers\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (page load)\n\n1. Browser parses `index.html`. The inline script in `<head>` reads `localStorage['theme']` and falls back to `prefers-color-scheme`, then sets `data-theme` on `<html>` before first paint (`index.html`).\n2. The module script `src/main.tsx` loads the variable font and `styles/index.css`, then renders `<App />` into `#root` (`src/main.tsx`). The static fallback markup in `#root` is replaced on mount.\n3. `App` renders `Providers` (`src/app/Providers.tsx`): `MotionPreferenceProvider` -> `MotionFeatures` -> `ToastProvider`.\n4. `Page` reads `siteContent` (`src/content/index.ts`), derives the single email address and the non-email contact links, and calls `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)` (`src/app/Page.tsx`).\n5. `Page` renders `SiteHeader` and `PillNav` (both receive `activeId` and `lockTo`), then the sections in order, then `Footer` (`src/app/Page.tsx`).\n6. `MotionFeatures` schedules a background import of `src/anim/features.ts` after first paint (`requestIdleCallback`, or a 1.5 s timer). It is skipped when reduced motion is on.\n\n### Scroll-spy\n\n1. `useActiveSection` creates one `IntersectionObserver` over the section ids, observing a thin band under the nav (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`).\n2. The observer sets `activeId`. A click on navigation calls `lockTo`, which holds the chosen id until scrolling settles.\n3. Desktop and phone navigation both read the same `activeId`, so there is one scroll-spy.\n\n### Theme toggle\n\n1. `ThemeToggle` (`src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`) gets `toggle` from `useTheme` (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`). That `toggle` calls `applyTheme` (`src/lib/theme.ts`).\n2. `applyTheme` writes `data-theme` first, then `localStorage`. If storage is blocked, it keeps an in-memory `unsavedChoice` flag.\n3. `subscribeTheme` uses a `MutationObserver` on `data-theme`, a `storage` listener for other tabs, and a `matchMedia` change listener for the OS scheme. It keeps `html[data-theme]` as the single source of truth.\n4. The visual thumb is `ThemeThumb` (`src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`), a react-spring component.\n\n### Project detail\n\n1. A click or tap on a project in `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx` or `Sleeve.tsx` sets a local `index` state.\n2. `ProjectDialog` (`src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`) calls `showModal()` in an effect and remembers the opener element. It reports close through `onIndexChange(null)`.\n3. Previous, Next, and unmodified ArrowLeft and ArrowRight move through projects with wrap-around.\n4. On close, the index returns to `null` and focus returns to the opener.\n\n### Copy email\n\n1. `useCopyEmail` (`src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`) is called by the Hero and Contact sections (`src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`, `src/sections/Contact/Contact.tsx`). It calls `copyText` (`src/lib/copy.ts`).\n2. `copyText` tries `navigator.clipboard.writeText`, then `document.execCommand('copy')` on a hidden textarea.\n3. If both fail, `openMail` assigns `window.location.href` to the `mailto:` link.\n4. Result text is shown through `useToast` (`src/components/ui/useToast.ts`) and the toast layer.\n\n### Build time\n\n```text\nsrc/content/*.ts --(typed, satisfies)--> src/app/Page.tsx --props--> sections --props--> layout, components/ui\nsrc/assets/images/* --(vite-imagetools, hashed imports)--> image fields in content\npublic/resume.pdf --(import.meta.env.BASE_URL)--> same-origin download links\n```\n\n**State Management:**\n- No global store. Three React contexts: `MotionPreferenceProvider`, `ToastProvider`, and the theme state read through `useTheme` (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`).\n- DOM state: `html[data-theme]` is the theme source of truth. `dialog.open` is the project-detail source of truth.\n- Module-level state: `unsavedChoice` in `src/lib/theme.ts`, and a features-loaded flag in `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts`.\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**Content record:**\n- Purpose: Typed description of one piece of portfolio content.\n- Examples: `src/content/schema.ts` (types `Profile`, `Project`, `ExperienceEntry`, `EducationEntry`, `SkillGroup`, `ContactLink`, `DateRange`, `ImageAsset`, `Writeup`)\n- Pattern: Each `src/content/<section>.ts` exports a typed array; `src/content/index.ts` assembles them. Ordering lives in data, not components.\n\n**Motion preference:**\n- Purpose: One place that decides whether animation runs.\n- Examples: `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`\n- Pattern: The provider configures `motion`'s `MotionConfig`, calls react-spring's `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation })`, and listens to the media query so live OS changes apply without a reload.\n\n**Semantic animation wrappers:**\n- Purpose: Hide library choice from sections.\n- Examples: `src/anim/declarative/ToastPresence.tsx`, `src/anim/declarative/NavIndicator.tsx`, `src/anim/declarative/PillMorph.tsx`, `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx`, `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`\n- Pattern: Each wrapper reads the motion preference, renders an instant equivalent when reduced, forwards `className`, and never hides content in its initial CSS state.\n\n**Lazy feature bundle:**\n- Purpose: Keep the initial JS under budget.\n- Examples: `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx` (loader and idle warm-up), `src/anim/features.ts` (`domMax` feature set)\n- Pattern: `LazyMotion` gets a loader promise. A failed load returns a promise that never settles, so the `m` components stay static and no unhandled rejection is raised.\n\n**Design token mirror:**\n- Purpose: One palette edit updates the whole UI.\n- Examples: `src/styles/tokens.css` (five role tokens per theme, derived tokens via `color-mix(in oklch, ...)`), `src/anim/tokens.ts` (spring values in motion and react-spring formats)\n- Pattern: Light and dark are authored separately. Tailwind v4 maps utilities through `@theme inline`. The dark variant is bound with `@custom-variant` to `[data-theme=dark]`.\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**Browser document:**\n- Location: `index.html` (main), `404.html` (not-found entry)\n- Triggers: Any navigation to the site. GitHub Pages serves `404.html` for unknown paths.\n- Responsibilities: Static fallback content, theme pre-paint script, module script `src/main.tsx`\n\n**Module entry:**\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: Module script in `index.html`\n- Responsibilities: Font import, global CSS import, `createRoot`, render `<App />` in `StrictMode`\n\n**Build entry:**\n- Location: `vite.config.ts`\n- Triggers: `npm run build`, `npm run dev`, `npm run test:e2e`\n- Responsibilities: `base` from `VITE_BASE`, React, Tailwind, imagetools plugins, `@` alias, two HTML inputs\n\n**Verification entry:**\n- Location: `package.json` script `verify`\n- Triggers: `npm run verify`, run by both GitHub Actions workflows\n- Responsibilities: Chains typecheck, lint, format check, unit tests, and the QA gates in `scripts/`\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers are used. The architecture doc states that pointer effects write react-spring values directly to the DOM without a React re-render per frame, which is the intended design.\n- **Global state:** Module-level mutable state in `src/lib/theme.ts` (`unsavedChoice`) and `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts` (features-loaded flag). The theme attribute on `document.documentElement` is shared state written by several modules.\n- **Circular imports:** None observed in `src/`.\n- **Single route:** No router. Adding a second page means adding a router, and `index.html` plus `404.html` are both static HTML entries. Read `docs/adr/0003-github-pages-source-and-cutover.md` before changing routing.\n- **Base path:** `VITE_BASE` sets the Vite `base`. Every reference to a file under `public/` must go through `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` (see `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`, `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`).\n- **Import boundaries:** `src/anim/` must not import sections, layout, or content. Only `src/app/Page.tsx` imports content data. These are lint errors.\n- **Lenis is not installed:** `docs/architecture.md` describes Lenis for desktop wheel smoothing. `lenis` is not in `package.json` and is not imported under `src/`. The current code has no smooth-scroll library.\n- **Native dialog:** Project detail uses `<dialog>` with `showModal()`, not a portal (`docs/adr/0009-native-dialog-for-project-details.md`).\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Hard-coded copy outside the content model\n\n**What happens:** `index.html` repeats hero copy as static markup inside `#root`. This includes the tagline, the graduation line, the \"Last role\" line, and a `mailto:` link with the email address.\n**Why it's wrong:** The content model says copy lives in `src/content/` and changes are recorded in the content inventory. The static copy can drift from `src/content/profile.ts` and `src/content/contacts.ts`. The no-JS fallback is intentional, but the duplication is not checked against the content tests.\n**Do this instead:** When editing hero or contact copy, update `src/content/*` first, then mirror the fallback in `index.html` in the same change. The fallback exists only for the no-JS and first-paint case.\n\n### Documented library that is not installed\n\n**What happens:** `docs/architecture.md` lists `lenis` (1.3.x) in the stack table and in the animation layer table. The package is absent from `package.json`, and no source file imports it.\n**Why it's wrong:** A planner reading the architecture doc may assume smooth wheel scrolling exists, or may add a second scroll library. The rule \"one library animates any given element\" still applies, so adding Lenis would need a new ADR.\n**Do this instead:** Treat Lenis as planned. Confirm `package.json` before depending on it. Use native scroll behavior and the `scroll-mt-(--nav-h)` class for anchor offsets (applied on section elements in `index.html` and the section components under `src/sections/`).\n\n### Duplicated theme-init logic\n\n**What happens:** The pre-paint theme script in `index.html` and the runtime logic in `src/lib/theme.ts` both read `localStorage['theme']` and `prefers-color-scheme`. The two implementations must agree on the storage key and fallback rules.\n**Why it's wrong:** A change in one place (for example, a new theme value) breaks the other silently. `tests/unit/theme-script.test.ts` exists to catch parity drift, so run it after changing either.\n**Do this instead:** Change `src/lib/theme.ts` and `index.html` together, then run `npx vitest run tests/unit/theme-script.test.ts`.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Fail soft. Browser APIs are wrapped in try/catch and fall back to a visible, working state rather than throwing.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- Storage access (`localStorage`) is wrapped in try/catch. Blocked storage keeps the choice in memory (`src/lib/theme.ts`).\n- `matchMedia` is wrapped. If missing, the OS scheme is not followed and the theme is light (`src/lib/theme.ts`). The scroll-spy hook also guards it (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`).\n- Clipboard failure falls back to `execCommand`, then to opening the mail client (`src/lib/copy.ts`).\n- A failed motion-feature chunk leaves animations static and raises no unhandled rejection (`src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`).\n- `IntersectionObserver` missing means scroll-spy does nothing and `activeId` stays `null` (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`).\n- There is no error boundary, and no runtime error reporting.\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None at runtime. Build and QA scripts log to the console.\n**Validation:** Compile-time only. `satisfies` against `src/content/schema.ts`, plus content unit tests (`tests/unit/content.test.ts`, `tests/unit/content-inventory.test.ts`). No runtime validation of data.\n**Authentication:** Not applicable.\n**Accessibility:** Skip link (`src/layout/SkipLink.tsx`), native dialog focus management, `prefers-reduced-motion` handling in `src/anim/` and `src/styles/base.css`, and axe checks in the QA loop.\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2129,"cache_read_input_tokens":86065,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2129,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_019ZJmZ9GMBeutVA1YbMdMai","agent_id":"a5cf52b35cb0bd10c","session_id":"dca2f8eb-cfce-4808-a3a2-6118fb064725","uuid":"f4f7e74a-53fe-4384-9500-0b0b7db52528","timestamp":"2026-11-20T04:52:53.087Z","request_id":"req_011ChDadU1fdNudyFQieaZEs","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Map tech and arch codebase"}

`*.spec.ts` under `tests/e2e/`\n- Styles: lowercase `.css` (`tokens.css`, `base.css`)\n\n**Directories:**\n- Section folders: PascalCase matching the section component (`src/sections/Hero/`)\n- Other source folders: lowercase (`src/lib/`, `src/anim/`, `src/components/ui/`)\n- Documentation ADRs: `docs/adr/NNNN-kebab-title.md`\n\n**Imports:**\n- Use the `@/` alias for anything under `src/` (`@/layout/Footer`, `@/anim`).\n- Animation: import from `@/anim` only. Never import `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` outside `src/anim/` and `src/components/motion-primitives/`.\n- Content data: import only in `src/app/Page.tsx`. Types may come from `@/content`.\n\n## Where to Add New Code\n\n**New content (copy, a project, a job):**\n- Data: `src/content/<section>.ts`, typed against `src/content/schema.ts`\n- Update `src/content/index.ts` only if you add a new top-level collection\n- Copy changes also need a record in `.planning/phases/01-decisions-and-documentation/01-CONTENT-INVENTORY.md` (see `docs/architecture.md`)\n- Images: add the file under `src/assets/images/`, register it in `src/content/images.ts`, and import it as a module (never reference it by a string path)\n\n**New section:**\n- Component: `src/sections/<Name>/<Name>.tsx`, taking data as props\n- Wire it into `src/app/Page.tsx` in the right order, and add its id to `SECTION_IDS` in `src/layout/nav-items.ts` so scroll-spy and nav include it\n- Tests: `tests/unit/layout.test.tsx` for structure, `tests/e2e/` for behavior\n- Do not import content data inside the section. Pass it from `Page.tsx`.\n\n**New shared UI component:**\n- Implementation: `src/components/ui/<Name>.tsx`\n- Use semantic Tailwind utilities (no raw hex or Tailwind palette colors). `scripts/check-colors.mjs` fails the build otherwise.\n\n**New icon:**\n- Implementation: `src/components/ui/icons/<Name>Icon.tsx` as inline SVG (see `docs/adr/0010-inline-svg-ui-glyphs.md`). Do not add an icon package.\n\n**New animation:**\n- Wrapper: `src/anim/declarative/` (motion) or `src/anim/physics/` (react-spring), exported through `src/anim/index.ts`\n- Spring and duration values: `src/anim/tokens.ts`\n- Respect the motion preference (instant render when reduced) and never hide content in the initial CSS state.\n\n**New hook:**\n- Implementation: `src/hooks/use<Name>.ts`\n\n**New pure helper:**\n- Implementation: `src/lib/<name>.ts`\n\n**New global style or token:**\n- Role colors and derived tokens: `src/styles/tokens.css` (light and dark blocks)\n- Tailwind mapping: `src/styles/index.css` (`@theme inline`)\n- Base or reset rules: `src/styles/base.css`\n- Component-scoped interaction styles: `src/styles/interactions.css`\n\n**New unit test:**\n- `tests/unit/<name>.test.ts` or `.test.tsx`. Fixtures that a gate test needs go in `tests/fixtures/`.\n\n**New e2e test:**\n- `tests/e2e/<name>.spec.ts`. Mobile-only specs go in `tests/e2e/mobile/`.\n\n**New QA gate script:**\n- `scripts/check-<name>.mjs`, then add it to the `verify` chain in `package.json` and, if it touches the built site, to the build-job order in `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` (the gate must run before the artifact is uploaded).\n\n**New documentation decision:**\n- `docs/adr/NNNN-<kebab-title>.md`, and list it in `docs/adr/README.md`.\n\n**Utilities:**\n- Shared helpers: `src/lib/utils.ts` (class composition with `cn`). Add new helpers to `src/lib/` rather than inside a section.\n\n## Special Directories\n\n**`.planning/`:**\n- Purpose: GSD planning state, phase plans and summaries, research, and the codebase map.\n- Generated: Partly (tooling writes some files)\n- Committed: Yes (tracked), except `.planning/milestone.lock`, which is git-ignored\n\n**`public/`:**\n- Purpose: Stable-URL static files. `resume.pdf` is checked by `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs`.\n- Generated: No\n- Committed: Yes\n\n**`src/assets/images/`:**\n- Purpose: Hashed-by-import images.
